### [1] [The Hidden Simplicity of the Classical Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31369)
*Yilber Fabian Bautista,Stefano De Angelis,David A. Kosower,Donal O'Connell,Matteo Sergola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在微扰论全阶、探针极限下研究了二对二大质量标量散射幅的经典极限，发现多重发射经典幅满足可由量子幅普通因式分解导出的简单因式分解，且经典幅可按加权树组织并进行递归计算，探针轨迹成为树的生成函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典力学与量子散射幅之间的联系是近年来散射幅研究的热点，但此前对二对二大质量标量幅的经典极限缺乏系统、全阶的理解。作者希望阐明多重发射经典幅的因式分解结构，并据此建立一种仅凭简单组合数学即可递归计算全阶经典幅的框架，超越逐阶硬算。

Method: 作者在探针极限下计算若干理论（一类标量理论、电动力学、含 half-BPS 物质的 N=8 超引力）的二对二大质量标量幅；证明多重发射经典幅满足一种简单因式分解，该因式分解可由量子幅的通常因式分解导出；进一步将多重发射与二对二经典幅组织为加权树，实现全阶递归计算；探针轨迹作为树的生成函数出现。边界条件由 Feynman iε 处方给出，从而引入一种新颖的世界线格林函数。

Result: 确立了经典因式分解关系（反映经典动力学的二阶性，且对含引力在内的更一般理论成立）；经典幅可用加权树递归生成并在探针极限下算至全阶；得到由 iε 处方定义的新世界线格林函数。

Conclusion: 复杂的经典动力学可归结为简单的组合结构（加权树与因式分解），从而在探针极限下对全阶经典幅实现系统化、可递归的计算，为把散射幅方法推广到一般经典散射问题提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study the classical limit of two-to-two massive-scalar amplitudes at all orders of perturbation theory. We compute these amplitudes in the probe limit for several theories: a family of scalar theories, electrodynamics, and $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity with half-BPS matter. We show that related multiple-emission classical amplitudes obey a simple factorization derivable from the usual factorization of quantum amplitudes. The classical factorization, which reflects the second-order nature of classical dynamics, holds in more general theories --- including gravity. We further show that both the multiple-emission and the two-to-two classical amplitudes have a simple organization in terms of weighted trees. This allows a recursive computation to all orders in the probe limit. The probe's trajectory emerges as a generating function of trees. The required boundary conditions are given by the Feynman $i \varepsilon$ prescription, leading to a novel worldline Green's function. In our approach, the rich classical dynamics arises from simple combinatorics.

</details>


### [2] [Bootstrapping two-loop $β$-functions for bosonic and heterotic strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31372)
*Achilleas Gitsis,Falk Hassler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用 O(d,d) 对称性而非直接计算来求非线性 σ 模型高阶 β 函数的方法，得到覆盖玻色弦与杂化弦的两圈 β 函数，并应用于可积 η 和 λ 形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性 σ 模型在一圈以上的 β 函数很难直接计算，需要寻找一种更高效、可推广到更高阶的方法。

Method: 将 β 函数视为低能有效作用量的梯度流，其中耦合空间度规仅由 O(d,d) 对称性确定，从而给出两参数族 O(d,d) 协变的两圈 β 函数。

Result: 得到一个同时覆盖玻色弦和杂化弦的两参数族 O(d,d) 协变两圈 β 函数；杂化弦的修正为玻色弦的一半。

Conclusion: 由于该方法只依赖对称性，为计算更高圈 β 函数开辟了新途径，并成功应用于 η 形变和 λ 形变的两圈重整化群流。

Abstract: Beyond one loop, $β$-functions of non-linear $σ$-models are hard to compute directly. We obtain them instead as a gradient flow of the low-energy effective action, whose coupling-space metric is fixed exclusively by symmetry. This gives a two-parameter family of O$(d,d)$-covariant, two-loop $β$-functions that covers the bosonic and heterotic string at once. Because only symmetry enters, the method opens a route to higher loops. As an application, we derive the two-loop RG flow of the integrable $η$- and $λ$-deformations; for the heterotic string, the correction is half of the bosonic one.

</details>


### [3] [A Kerr-black-hole worldline action to $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31377)
*Yilber Fabian Bautista,Mathias Driesse,Kays Haddad,Gustav Uhre Jakobsen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过有效场论计算引力波从旋转致密天体（黑洞）散射的四圈振幅，构建了一个世界线作用量，该作用量在物理后闵可夫斯基计数下可精确重现克尔黑洞动力学至牛顿常数五阶（含黎曼曲率张量二阶、自旋张量四阶的算符），并成功与线性黑洞微扰论匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在后闵可夫斯基近似下高精度地描述旋转黑洞（克尔黑洞）的散射动力学，特别是吸收效应与保守自旋效应，以便为双体散射和引力波模板提供准确的世界线有效作用量。

Method: 采用有效场论（EFT）方法，计算引力波从旋转致密天体散射的振幅至四圈阶；将该结果与线性黑洞微扰论的预言进行匹配，确定世界线作用量中所需的高阶曲率算符（含时间反演反对称的吸收算符与保守的二次自旋算符），并将作用量构建至牛顿常数五阶。

Result: 成功建立一个至五阶后闵可夫斯基的世界线作用量，包含黎曼曲率二次方、自旋四次方的算符；匹配所需的二次曲率算符包括线性与三次自旋阶的时间反演反对称项（刻画视界吸收）以及二次自旋保守项。作为应用，计算出双体散射中克尔黑洞自旋大小变化的领头阶，并重现质量变化的领头阶，适用于任意自旋取向。

Conclusion: 该世界线作用量能够正确重现克尔黑洞动力学至第五阶牛顿常数，统一描述了散射中的保守与耗散（吸收）效应，并可直接用于计算自旋与质量在散射过程中的变化，为高精度引力波物理提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We establish a worldline action which correctly reproduces Kerr-black-hole dynamics up to fifth order in Newton's constant in a physical post-Minkowskian counting. This encompasses operators up to second order in the Riemann curvature tensor and up to fourth order in the spin tensor. Doing so entails performing the full effective-field-theory computation of the amplitude for a gravitational-wave scattering off of a spinning compact object up to four-loop order, and matching the result to the predictions from linear black hole perturbation theory. Quadratic-in-curvature operators which must be added to match successfully include time-reversal anti-symmetric ones at linear and cubic spin orders, capturing absorption across the black hole horizon, and conservative quadratic-in-spin ones. As an immediate application of our action, we compute the leading-order change in spin magnitude, and reproduce the leading change in mass, for a Kerr black hole in a scattering event with a second compact body, considering a generic orientation of the black hole's spin.

</details>


### [4] [Circulating currents around orbifolded defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31391)
*Matthew M. Roberts*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究具有五维 orbifold 奇点的全息 monodromy 缺陷，它们在十维中提升为光滑且超对称的几何，并与 spindle 紧化及广义 Gukov-Witten 缺陷相关。作者计算了单点函数与反常系数，并发现即使所有 monodromy 关闭，缺陷仍表现出非零环流，对应 1/8-BPS 激发态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来 spindle 紧化中的五维 orbifold 奇点几何备受关注，被解读为广义 Gukov-Witten 缺陷。作者旨在研究这类缺陷的构造、物理性质及其与真空（empty anti-de Sitter）的绝热联系，从而揭示其作为激发态缺陷的独特行为。

Method: 通过在一个具有圆锥超额的角（conical excess angle）空间上对已有的全息 monodromy 缺陷取 orbifold，构造提升为光滑、超对称的十维几何。orbifold 作用同时非平凡地作用于五维基空间和五维球面。随后计算单点函数和反常系数（A 型与 B 型）作为 monodromy 与 orbifold 数据的函数，并比较不同体拓扑下的缺陷。

Result: 计算发现：即使所有 monodromy 关闭，缺陷仍具有非零环流，因而与绝热连接到真空的缺陷不同，对应 1/8-BPS 激发态；这类缺陷不与空 anti-de Sitter 真空绝热相连。比较体拓扑后发现：非平凡拓扑缺陷的 A 型反常系数总是大于平凡拓扑缺陷，而 B 型系数则可大可小。

Conclusion: 带有五维 orbifold 奇点的全息 monodromy 缺陷构成一类基于非平凡体拓扑的 1/8-BPS 激发态缺陷。它们即使在 monodromy 关闭时也保持非零环流，且 A 型反常系数严格大于平凡拓扑情形，而 B 型系数没有普遍单调性，揭示了拓扑对缺陷反常的差异化影响。

Abstract: We investigate holographic monodromy defects with five dimensional orbifold singularities which lift to smooth and supersymmetric ten dimensional geometries. Such geometries have been of recent interest in spindle compactifications and have been interpreted as generalized Gukov-Witten defects. They are constructed by taking an orbifold of previously considered holographic monodromy defects on a space with a conical excess angle, and are not adiabatically connected to the empty anti-de Sitter vacuum. The the orbifold action acts nontrivially both on the five dimensional base as well as the five-sphere. We calculate one-point functions and anomaly coefficients as a function of the monodromies and orbifold data. Surprisingly, we discover that defects exhibit nonzero circulating currents even when all monodromies are turned off, unlike defects adiabatically connected to the vacuum, and therefore correspond to 1/8-BPS excited states. Comparing defects with nontrivial bulk topology we find that the A-type anomaly coefficient is always larger than those with trivial topology, while the B-type coefficient can be either larger or smaller.

</details>


### [5] [The Schwarzschild-Kerr crossover of high overtone quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31394)
*Yasuyuki Hatsuda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究缓慢旋转黑洞的高阻尼准正规模式谱，发现Schwarzschild近似仅在泛音阶数约χ^{-4/3}以内成立；该尺度上的频率偏移为一普适函数，可由六次振子谱给出，并恢复Schwarzschild的log 3结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓慢旋转黑洞虽接近非旋转黑洞，但其高阻尼准正规谱并非如此。作者旨在刻画Schwarzschild近似失效的临界泛音阶数，并给出该尺度上频率偏移的普适描述，以澄清慢旋转近似在准正规谱中的适用范围与修正结构。

Method: 作者利用波动方程边界条件推导频率偏移，建立常微分方程与可积模型之间的对应关系，将问题转化为一个六次振子的谱问题（sextic oscillator spectrum），从而得到普适的频率偏移函数，并附有误差估计。

Result: Schwarzschild近似仅对泛音阶数在χ^{-4/3}量级以内成立；在此尺度上频率偏移为一普适函数，由六次振子谱给出；该结果恢复Schwarzschild的log 3频率虚部，并系统整理了Kerr谱中的奇异系数。

Conclusion: 缓慢旋转黑洞的高阻尼准正规谱在特定泛音阶数尺度上偏离Schwarzschild结果，其频率偏移具有普适性，可由六次振子的谱解析描述，从而统一了Schwarzschild极限与Kerr的奇异系数结构。

Abstract: A slowly rotating black hole is close to a nonrotating one, but its highly damped quasinormal spectrum is not. The Schwarzschild approximation holds only up to overtone numbers of order $χ^{-4/3}$, where $χ$ is the dimensionless spin. We find that the frequency shift at this scale is a universal function, derived from the wave boundary conditions with an error estimate. It is given by the spectrum of a sextic oscillator, via the correspondence between ordinary differential equations and integrable models. It recovers the Schwarzschild $\log 3$ and organizes the singular Kerr coefficients.

</details>


### [6] [Refined Rényi Entropy from Replica-Geometry Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31398)
*Qiang Wen,Haocheng Zhong,Yiwei Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类依赖副本指标(replica index)的测地线图张量网络，在保持链接最大纠缠的同时，通过几何方式重现宇宙膜(cosmic brane)对精细Rényi熵的n依赖性，并在CFT₂中验证了边界重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准固定图最大纠缠张量网络的前沿纠缠谱平坦，导致Rényi熵不依赖副本指标n，这与全息共形场论中由张力宇宙膜导致的n依赖行为矛盾。捕捉这一副本依赖性是对张量网络描述全息纠缠的重要检验。

Method: 作者构造一族显式依赖副本指标的测地线图，链接保持最大纠缠。对每个副本指标n，测地线以相同的局部各向同性交集测度从复制边界发出，完美铺砌所选副本几何，使交集计数的n依赖性完全来自几何。在图上构建的张量网络模型通过数穿越线来重现宇宙膜对精细Rényi熵的处方。

Result: 这些图上的张量网络模型通过计数穿越线重现了宇宙膜对精细Rényi熵的处方；其跨副本端点密度在CFT₂中与加性线性组合方案内的边界重建一致，提供了超越总熵的检验；在高维情形下还得到了该密度的大间隔解析衰减律。

Conclusion: 通过让副本几何决定交集计数，该构造成功解决了固定图张量网络中Rényi熵缺乏副本依赖的结构性障碍，为用张量网络描述全息精细纠缠提供了可行的几何框架，并给出了可检验的边界重建结果。

Abstract: Holographic tensor networks are a central route to understanding the mechanism behind the holographic duality, but standard fixed-graph constructions with maximally entangled links face a structural obstacle. Their leading entanglement spectra are flat, so their Rényi entropies do not depend on the replica index. This is in sharp contrast with holographic conformal field theories, where a tensionful cosmic brane makes the refined Rényi entropy $n$-dependent. Capturing this replica dependence is therefore an important test of tensor-network descriptions of holographic entanglement. We construct an explicit family of replica-dependent geodesic graphs that reproduces this behavior while keeping the links maximally entangled. For each replica index $n$, geodesics are emitted from the replicated boundary with the same local, isotropic intersection measure. They perfectly tessellate the selected replica geometry, so the $n$-dependence of their intersection counts comes from the geometry. Tensor-network models on these graphs reproduce the cosmic-brane prescription for refined Rényi entropy by counting crossing threads. Their cross-copy endpoint density also agrees, in CFT$_2$, with a boundary reconstruction within the additive linear combination proposal, providing a check beyond the total entropy. In higher dimensions, we further obtain an analytic large-separation decay law for this density.

</details>


### [7] [Elliptic spin Ruijsenaars--Schneider model via the Poisson reduction of the affine Heisenberg double](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31405)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Lukas Hardi,Davide Polvara*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过对仿射Heisenberg double（扩展一个仿射振子流形）做Poisson约化，构造了带动力学非齐次性(inhomogeneities)的椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars–Schneider模型的Hamilton结构，将该方法从双曲情形推广到椭圆情形，并建立了Poisson约化与necklace quiver椭圆Coulomb branch之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋Ruijsenaars–Schneider模型的Heisenberg double构造此前仅在双曲情形有效，椭圆情形尚缺乏相应的Hamilton结构构造；同时需要解释该结构与近来从necklace quiver椭圆Coulomb branch得到的Poisson括号和Hamilton量为何一致。

Method: 以扩展了仿射振子流形的仿射Heisenberg double为相空间，通过Poisson约化构造Hamilton结构；求解矩映射约束(moment map constraint)，导出不变Lax算符与自旋变量的Poisson代数；通过在仿射参数的不同点处求值并局域化不变振子，得到有限自由度实现，并要求这些点为动力学变量以抵消导数项、保证Poisson括号在有限自由度上闭合。

Result: 得到了带动力学非齐次性的椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars–Schneider模型的Poisson括号与Hamilton量，且解析结果与necklace quiver椭圆Coulomb branch最近得到的结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 该构造为这一Hamilton结构提供了独立推导，把Heisenberg double方法推广到椭圆情形，并建立了Poisson约化与Coulomb branch之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We construct the Hamiltonian structure of the elliptic spin Ruijsenaars--Schneider model with dynamical inhomogeneities by Poisson reduction of the affine Heisenberg double extended by an affine oscillator manifold. This construction extends the Heisenberg double approach to spin Ruijsenaars--Schneider models from the hyperbolic to the elliptic case. We solve the moment map constraint and derive the Poisson algebras of the invariant Lax operator and spin variables. A realization with finitely many degrees of freedom is obtained by evaluating and localizing the invariant oscillators at distinct points of the affine parameter. Consistency requires these points to be dynamical so that their nontrivial Poisson brackets with the spin variables cancel derivative terms that would otherwise obstruct closure of the Poisson bracket on finitely many degrees of freedom. The resulting Poisson brackets and Hamiltonian coincide with those recently obtained from the elliptic Coulomb branch of the necklace quiver. Our construction thus provides an independent derivation of this Hamiltonian structure and establishes a direct link between Poisson reductions and Coulomb branches.

</details>


### [8] [Flat Space Entanglement from Wedge Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31414)
*Robert C. Myers,Bruno Sivilotti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维平坦时空的全息纠缠熵，将楔形全息与AdS世界末日膜的受控平坦极限相结合，发现区间纠缠熵取二维Carrollian CFT的泛函形式，但中心荷在平坦极限下出现c_M=0、c_L→∞的奇异行为；加入引力Chern-Simons项后仍无法改善，且极值面塌缩到零无穷远上的零射线而非Song等人提出的swing surface。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索渐近平坦三维时空的全息对偶（Carrollian CFT）中的纠缠熵结构，检验现有关于swing surface的提议，并揭示平坦极限下中心荷所表现出的病态行为是否具有一般性。

Method: 结合楔形全息与受控的AdS世界末日膜平坦极限：将两个AdS3膜所围的AdS4楔形取极限，得到双平坦膜的Randall-Sundrum型构型；计算二维角点CFT中区间的纠缠熵，并考察加入引力Chern-Simons项后中心荷与极值面的变化。

Result: 得到的区间纠缠熵具有二维Carrollian CFT的泛函形式，但推出的中心荷满足c_M=0、c_L→∞；加入Chern-Simons项能产生类c_M贡献，但平坦极限下仍为c_M→0、c_L→∞。极值面并不趋近Song等人提出的swing surface，而是塌缩到零无穷远上的零射线。

Conclusion: 中心荷奇异性与极值面塌缩是AdS/CFT平坦极限的一般特征，源于纠缠熵是依赖域的性质以及真空中区间因果楔与纠缠楔的相等。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in three-dimensional flat space by combining wedge holography with a controlled flat limit of AdS end-of-the-world branes. The limit takes a wedge of AdS$_4$ bounded by two AdS$_3$ branes to a Randall-Sundrum-like configuration of two flat branes, and the entanglement entropy of an interval in the two-dimensional corner CFT becomes that of an interval at null infinity of the branes. The result has the functional form of the entanglement entropy of a two-dimensional Carrollian CFT, the proposed holographic dual of gravity in asymptotically flat three-dimensional spacetimes. However, the inferred central charges yield $c_M=0$ and $c_L\to\infty$ in the flat limit. Adding a gravitational Chern-Simons term produces a $c_M$-like contribution, but we still find $c_L\to\infty$ and $c_M\to0$ in this limit. Moreover, the corresponding extremal surfaces do not approach the swing surfaces proposed by Song and collaborators. Instead, they collapse onto null rays along null infinity. We argue that both features are generic for flat limits of AdS/CFT. They follow from entanglement entropy being a property of the domain of dependence and from the equality of causal and entanglement wedges for intervals in the vacuum.

</details>


### [9] [A spin chain as a deformed conformal field theory: an ODE/IM approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31417)
*Sujay K. Ashok,Nicolò Primi,Roberto Tateo*

Main category: hep-th

Their finite-size corrections are expected to come from the Lee--Yang conformal field theory deformed by irrelevant operators, and with the help of the ODE/IM correspondence we make this deformation explicit for the energy and the local conserved charges. The two chiralities are not independent: finite-size effects couple them, so that the charges of each shift the chemical potentials of the other. Conventional critical behavior is examined through the criticality conditions for the Hawking temperature in both canonical and grand canonical ensembles. We then use thermodynamic topology to investigate the global structure of equilibrium configurations and its dependence on the thermodynamic parameters. In the canonical ensemble, two distinct regimes emerge: a noncritical region characterized by a total winding number \(W=+1\), and a critical region with \(W=0\). Interestingly, the low- and intermediate-potential regions share the same topological number, \(W=+1\), although conventional critical points occur only in the intermediate-potential region. The high-potential region, by contrast, is characterized by \(W=0\) and exhibits no conventional critical behavior. Our findings show that thermodynamic topology provides a global characterization of equilibrium configurations that complements conventional temperature-based criticality analysis, and that the global winding number alone does not uniquely determine the existence of conventional critical points.

This prunes the tower to finitely many modes, all damped faster than $Δ^2E_b$, and below $E=Δ^2E_b/2$ none is left. The $t^{-2Δ}$ decay, which is the boundary counterpart of the Aretakis growth, ends by $t\sim1/E_b$, when the throat ceases to be classical, and by then horizon derivatives have grown only to a finite value. Abstract: We study a massive field strongly mixed with the curvature perturbation during inflation, in the regime where the latter is described at low energies by a single-field effective field theory (EFT) with a quadratic dispersion relation. We solve it with a general method that does not rely on an exact solution. An expansion in the inverse mixing, keeping both fields, yields the EFT as its leading order, with corrections under systematic control. Bosonization identifies momentum-winding T-duality, with a parity-projected operator flowing to a noninvertible topological defect. A nonperturbative norm-resolvent bound proves emergent dynamical gauge locality at finite chemical potential, while a doubled Chern-insulator parent reveals the duality's bulk origin and superconducting extensions reveal hidden compact symmetries.

Relocalization retains Schwartz locality for asymptotically local gauging, while AIII/AII parents clarify bulk charge origin and parity-anomaly inflow. Two flavors yield compact $U(2)$, a quaternionic invariant, and asymptotically local non-Abelian gauging.
  In 3D, an exact mixed vector-axial flux response is derived, while a distinct model supports an exponentially local vector $U(1)$ charge with strong quaternionic topology. A nonlocal map makes the charge and Kramers symmetry onsite while preserving exponential Hamiltonian locality. For a compact $SU(2)$ Weyl doublet, a no-go theorem forbids translation-preserving Gaussian onsite relocalization retaining Weyl content and Hamiltonian locality.

Abstract: High harmonic generation (HHG) in solids typically produces a rigid frequency comb locked to exact integer multiples of the drive photon energy. In strongly correlated materials, however, the light field can dynamically transform the electronic state on the timescale of harmonic generation itself, potentially violating this rule. Here, we report giant blueshifted, non-integer HHG in a cuprate Mott insulator Sr$_2$CuO$_3$ produced by mid-infrared excitations. Concurrent transient reflectivity measurements and time-dependent exact-diagonalization simulations of a driven Hubbard model support a many-body temporal gating mechanism that suppresses light emission on the trailing edge of the pulse, thereby blueshifting radiated harmonics in the frequency domain. Abstract: Despite extensive experimental and theoretical studies of quadrupolar order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$, the microscopic origin and relative energy scales of the interactions driving it remain unresolved. Here, we determine all these interactions from first principles and solve the resulting microscopic Hamiltonian. The calculated ground state reproduces the experimentally observed quadrupolar order parameters and the associated structural distortions with quantitative accuracy. Disentangling the individual contributions, we find that ferroic $z^2$ order originates from a linear-quartic coupling, generated by virtual excitations to the $J_{\mathrm{eff}}$= 1/2 doublet and driven by vibronic interactions. Abstract: The spin-1/2 XXZ antiferromagnet on the triangular lattice in a magnetic field is a paradigmatic frustrated quantum magnet, yet its ground-state phase diagram at generic anisotropy and its thermal phase structure remain incompletely understood. Here, using infinite density-matrix renormalization group, infinite projected entangled-pair operator, and exponential tensor renormalization group methods, supported by exact diagonalization, we resolve both issues. At zero temperature, we map the phase diagram continuously from the Ising limit to the Heisenberg point and find that the nonclassical $Ψ$ supersolid is a stable phase just below saturation at all anisotropies studied: it borders the one-third-magnetization up-up-down plateau directly in the strongly easy-axis regime and the semiclassical $V$ phase toward the Heisenberg point. In the strongly easy-axis regime, we uncover an intrinsically finite-temperature bond-nematic phase above the zero-temperature plateau--$Ψ$ boundary, in which the three bond directions become spontaneously inequivalent without transverse spin coherence or three-sublattice order. A hard-core-boson mapping reveals its origin: once thermal fluctuations destroy phase coherence, the field-doped holes settle on domain walls of the local up-up-down order, which are stabilized by quantum kink delocalization and aligned by its $Z_3$ registry. Our results call for reexamining high-field states in triangular-lattice cobaltates such as Na$_2$BaCo(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Co(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者重新考察用于估算 D 的三类费曼图贡献：裸环图（bare rings）、含一个一阶交换插入的环图（exchange rings）、以及具有三个不同动量转移的三阶图。重点在于正确处理交换环图的耦合常数加权（coupling-constant weighting），并计算更高阶 rs² 项的贡献。

Conclusion: 均匀电子气关联能展开中的线性系数 D 应修正为 -0.0077880(9) Ha/电子，主要纠正来自交换环图的耦合常数加权。尽管现有扩散蒙特卡洛数据在 rs=0.5 尚不能区分新旧估计，但该修正为密度泛函理论关联泛函的构建提供了更精确的高密度展开系数。

Abstract: The high-density expansion of the correlation energy per electron of the unpolarized three-dimensional electron gas, $ε_c = A\ln r_s + B + C r_s\ln r_s + D r_s + \dots$, is used in many approximations to the correlation functional of density-functional theory. The coefficients $A$, $B$ and $C$ are known exactly. We revisit the three diagram classes used to estimate $D$, namely bare rings, rings with one first-order exchange insertion, and third-order diagrams with three distinct momentum transfers. Their sum is $-0.0077880(9)$ Ha per electron, compared with the earlier estimate of $-0.010$ Ha. The difference comes mainly from the coupling-constant weighting of the exchange rings in the calculation of Carr and Maradudin. At order $r_s^2$, seven contributions to the single-logarithm coefficient $E_1$ sum to $(3.653 \pm 0.010)\times 10^{-3}$ Ha per electron. The three long-wavelength contributions to the double-logarithm coefficient $E_2$ sum to $α^2(36π^2 - 208\ln 2 - 221)/(192π^4) \approx -1.433\times 10^{-4}$ Ha per electron, where $α= (4/9π)^{1/3}$. Available diffusion Monte Carlo energies at $r_s = 0.5$ cannot distinguish between the two estimates of $D$.

Result: fQKS 显著优于 QKS，且与 IQP、PQC 在统计上无显著差异；fPQC 优于 IQP 和 QKS，并在 28 个配置中与 PQC 打平。在更难的大规模文本和图像任务上，费米子能量基分类器在图像上超越主流的 QML 模型，在文本上与它们持平或略逊，全程接近经典基线。

Conclusion: 模型性能对架构高度敏感，但 FermiML 构造的费米子代理能够挑战甚至超越其不受约束的对应 QML 模型，并能将费米子 QML 模型的规模扩展到真实世界数据集上的经典基线水平，说明部分所谓 QML 优势并非来自真正的量子资源。

Abstract: We present a framework for building and benchmarking scalable machine learning (ML) models based on simulating free-fermion systems. Our fermionic ML (FermiML) framework is (i) versatile enough to encompass kernel methods and neural networks, (ii) applicable to classification and regression, and (iii) scalable to real-world datasets. FermiML models are quantum circuits sharing the building blocks of most quantum ML (QML) models: (parameterized) qubit rotations and entangling gates. This allows us to build fermionic surrogates that mimic the architecture, and hence the quantum resources and inductive bias, of any QML model while remaining efficiently classically simulable. We thus benchmark QML models against close fermionic counterparts on equal footing and ascertain whether a purported QML gain truly stems from classically inaccessible resources or is an accidental side-effect. Across 31 configurations from five classification datasets, we benchmark quantum kitchen sinks (QKS), instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) and generic parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) models against fermionic surrogates. fQKS significantly outperforms QKS and is statistically indistinguishable from IQP and PQC; fPQC outperforms IQP and QKS and ties PQC in 28 configurations. We also introduce fermionic fidelity and Pfaffian covariance kernels for regression and establish QML baselines for this harder task. Finally, we introduce a fermionic energy-based classifier scalable to large text and image datasets, which outperforms prominent QML models on images, beats or closely trails them on text, and stays close to classical baselines throughout. Our results show that while performance depends sensitively on architecture, FermiML yields fermionic surrogates that challenge or outperform their unrestricted counterparts and fermionic QML models that match the scale of classical baselines on real-world datasets.

</details>

The Co-O exchange is found to strongly enhance the critical temperature in the mean field approximation but not in the random phase approximation.

Abstract: The hexagonal altermagnet $α$-MnTe combines a near-room-temperature collinear order with a strongly spin-split electronic structure, yet the microscopic origin of its easy-plane anisotropy, its zone-center magnon gap and its anomalously large magnetostriction have remained unexplained. The isotropic exchange, including the altermagnetic splitting of the tenth-neighbor coupling, agrees with inelastic neutron scattering data. Contrary to the common single-ion picture, we find that the easy-plane anisotropy and the magnon gap at $Γ$ are governed by classical dipole-dipole interactions, which enhance the zone-center magnon splitting threefold leading to quantitative agreement with experiment, while the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya term is irrelevant despite its size. The same Hamiltonian reproduces the chiral magnon splitting along antinodal directions, mirroring the electronic spin splitting, and quantitatively accounts for the giant magnetovolume coupling of $α$-MnTe through the volume dependence of superexchange.

Abstract: Following the real-time dynamics of correlated electrons to extended times, at all interaction strengths, remains an open challenge. We address it with an adaptive time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation (td-gGA). The method extends the Gutzwiller approximation by auxiliary ghost orbitals. It adds more of them whenever the correlations that build up during the dynamics exceed a threshold. This is computationally far more efficient than keeping their number fixed. As a demonstration we take the interaction quench of the half-filled Hubbard model on the Bethe lattice. For this paradigmatic problem, numerically exact nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) serves as the reference. A previous td-gGA study with a fixed set of 6 ghost orbitals per spin largely reproduced the available DMFT data. It lacked, however, an error estimate that would support predictions beyond them. With up to 16 ghost orbitals per spin, adaptive td-gGA agrees with DMFT wherever DMFT data are available, from the weak-coupling prethermal regime to the strong-coupling collapse-and-revival regime. At strong coupling it remains converged up to three and a half times as long as the DMFT data. Near the crossover between the two regimes, the system relaxes to thermal values that agree with DMFT. Resolving the dynamics in the number of ghost orbitals shows how the sharp dynamical transition of the Gutzwiller approximation turns into this smooth crossover. In the extended time window at strong coupling, we observe that the oscillation frequency is renormalized as predicted by our second-order strong-coupling expansion. With its built-in error estimate, adaptive td-gGA offers a systematically improvable route to the extended-time dynamics of driven and multi-orbital correlated systems, especially when combined with tensor-network or quantum solvers for the embedding problem.

Abstract: We study the spectrum of arrays of critical transverse field Ising chains perturbed by a weak inter-chain coupling in the scaling limit, using an exact diagonalization algorithm based on Hamiltonian truncation. As the number of chains is made large, we confirm the existence of excitations below the two particle continuum that can be identified with the celebrated Zamalodchikov $E_8$ theory with small corrections. In addition, we find a distinct $ψ$-particle, with mass $M_\mathrmψ$ which is not contained in the $E_8$ theory. Its ratio to the lowest $E_8$ mass ($M_1$) is a universal number, which we have computed numerically to be $M_\mathrmψ/M_1 = 1.819\cdots$ for a one dimensional array of critical chains. We present evidence that this new state fundamentally involves excitations on neighboring chains, explaining why it has no analogue in the single chain problem. Interestingly the $M_\mathrmψ$ mass has a characteristic scaling dependence on detuning from the critical point that is qualitatively distinct from the $E_8$ masses, which can help identify it in spectroscopic experiments on easy-axis magnetic chain materials.

Abstract: We investigate the elastic response of cubic NiCoCr$_{0.8}$, a quantum-critical medium-entropy alloy, by high-precision ultrasound velocity measurements. The tetragonal $E_g$ mode $C_t$ softens with increasing field, whereas the derived bulk $A_{1g}$ mode $C_B$ hardens, revealing an opposite field response between the two strain-symmetry channels. Comparison with the previously reported quantum-critical-Fermi-liquid crossover scale shows that the field-induced elastic renormalization develops already in the quantum-critical regime and evolves continuously across the crossover region, without a sharp elastic anomaly or threshold behavior. These results demonstrate that symmetry-resolved ultrasound provides a sensitive probe of subtle strain-dependent changes in the electronic and magnetic free energy of a chemically disordered itinerant quantum-critical metal.

Then we discuss modern fabrication and characterization techniques that make analysis of a sample's topology not only an interesting theoretical concept but also a directly accessible and practically relevant topic. The main attention of the Chapter is dedicated to the theoretical and experimental analysis of nanostructrures with the topology different from the extended films beyond the in-depth studied topic of tubes of the constant radius. Abstract: The multichannel fluctuating field (MCFF) approach treats selected collective fluctuations in interacting quantum lattice models nonperturbatively, but has so far been applied only to single-band systems. Here, we extend MCFF theory to multiorbital systems, accounting for the orbital structure of charge and spin instabilities. We apply it to the half-filled two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model with repulsive intra-orbital interaction $U$, and both positive and negative Hund's coupling $J$, addressing the competing orders and their interplay. The ability to selectively retain individual fluctuation channels and control their interplay allows us to disentangle their respective contributions and identify the mechanisms underlying competing instabilities. The model features a simple competition between charge density wave and antiferromagnetic fluctuations in the vicinity of $J=U$, and a more intricate competition between antiferromagnetic, orbital, and spin-orbital fluctuations near $J=0$, where the model acquires a higher SU(4) symmetry. The proposed multiorbital extension thus provides a computationally inexpensive and efficient framework for exploring how orbital and spin character shape competing collective fluctuations.

Conclusion: NV自旋噪声谱可作为α-RuCl₃纳米薄片中低能自旋动力学的局域、非侵入式探针，并为在原子级薄QSL候选材料中检验分数化激发预测噪声特征开辟了途径。

Abstract: Bond-dependent magnetic interactions, strong spin-orbit coupling, and quantum fluctuations in $α$-RuCl$_3$ make it an archetypical candidate for exotic Kitaev quantum spin liquid (QSL) states. While electronic transport, neutron scattering, optical and tunneling spectroscopies were used to probe global QSL signatures in large scale $α$-RuCl$_3$ samples, nanoscale evidence of local QSL behavior via a non-invasive experimental probe is still missing. Here, we use diamond nitrogen-vacancy (NV) spin-noise spectroscopy to probe QSL states in α-RuCl3 nanoflakes with thicknesses ranging from 8 to 150 nm over a temperature range of 4.3-250 K. Our measurements reveal a pronounced decrease in the NV spin-lattice relaxation time ($T_{1}$) below ~100 K in proximity to the $α$-RuCl$_3$ flakes, indicating enhanced GHz-frequency magnetic fluctuations. This temperature scale is consistent with the energy scale associated with Kitaev interactions (~8 meV) and the emergence of low-energy fractionalized excitations. Below 50 K, thicker (> 90 nm) flakes exhibit a distinct bi-exponential $T_{1}$ decay, unlike the single-exponential behavior observed for off-flake and thinner (8 nm) flake measurements, suggesting an increasingly heterogeneous magnetic noise environment. These results establish NV spin-noise spectroscopy as a local, non-invasive probe of low-energy spin dynamics in $α$-RuCl$_3$ nanoflakes and open a route to testing predicted noise signatures of fractionalized excitations in atomically thin QSL candidates.

Abstract: Resolving the thermodynamic entropy associated with a local impurity in a spatially extended interacting quantum bath remains a numerical challenge. We develop a Local-Maxwell approach that reconstructs the impurity entropy from local observables in worldline quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations. By integrating their temperature responses along the impurity coupling, the method avoids separate estimates of extensive entropies. For nonnegative worldline expansions with bounded local couplings, we establish bath-size independent bounds on all factorial moments of the impurity vertex count. For even vertex counts, the Maxwell estimator variance remains bounded as the coupling approaches zero. The transverse-field Ising benchmark reproduces exact free fermion results, while an interacting XXZ chain demonstrates up to several hundredfold improvement in statistical efficiency over conventional extensive thermodynamic integration. In a three-channel XXZ impurity junction, we reveal entropy recovery upon cooling and a nonmonotonic coupling dependence of the finite-temperature entropy contrast. These results establish local thermodynamic reconstruction as an efficient approach to impurity entropy in interacting quantum baths.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 EA 自旋玻璃基态的稳定性及其多重基态对的存在性。此前已排除第一种 σ-临界性，本文旨在排除剩余的第二种 σ-临界性，以消除多重基态对出现的一个充分条件，并提出其必要条件。

Result: 证明第二种 σ-临界性在 EA 自旋玻璃中也不存在，从而完全排除了任意维度下 σ-临界性的存在，并提出了基态对多重性的一个严格必要条件。

Conclusion: σ-临界性在 EA 自旋玻璃中完全不存在；这虽未解决基态对多重性问题，但移除了其一个充分条件，并给出了严格的必要条件，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The stability of ground states in the Edwards-Anderson Ising spin glass in dimensions two and higher against perturbations of a single coupling can be quantified through the ``flexibility'' of that coupling in a particular ground state. The property of $σ$-criticality is one in which varying a single coupling can alter the flexibilities of a (strictly) positive density of couplings in an infinite system, and its presence provides a sufficient condition for the presence of multiple ground state pairs. In a previous paper it was shown for the EA spin glass that $σ$-criticality of the first kind, in which the critical droplet of a bond has a positive-density interface with the ground state, was ruled out in any dimension. The remaining possibility, called $σ$-criticality of the second kind, is one in which a bond lies in the critical droplet boundaries of a positive density of other bonds. We show here that this kind of $σ$-criticality is also absent, ruling out altogether the presence of $σ$-criticality in the EA spin glass. While this doesn't resolve the question of multiplicity of ground state pairs, it removes a sufficient condition for its presence. We then propose a rigorous necessary condition for ground state pair multiplicity.

</details>

Method: 采用重整化群方法：将安德森局域化问题置于两个 RG 不动点碰撞并变为复数的“共形性丧失”框架下；引入类 BKT 的双参数标度流描述可观测量；并以 ε=1/d≪1 为小参数对 RG 方程进行 ε 展开，构造唯象 RG 方程。

Conclusion: 高维安德森局域化的标度行为可由“共形性丧失”机制下的类 BKT 双参数流描述，且 1/d 展开提供了与数值一致的有效描述，验证了此前关于双参数标度流的猜想。

Abstract: We show that the recent works on Anderson localization when the number of spatial dimensions $d\to\infty$ can be framed into the scenario dubbed it conformality lost [Kaplan et al., Phys. Rev. D 80, 125005 (2009)], which occurs when two fixed points of the RG collide and become complex. This framing makes it natural for a two-parameter scaling flow à la Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) to control all the observables of the theory as conjectured in [Vanoni et al., PNAS 121, e2401955121 (2024)]. We also use this as a starting point for an $ε$ expansion of the RG equations in $ε=1/d\ll 1$, and find phenomenological RG equations which fit the numerical results in dimensions $d\gtrsim 4$.

</details>

Abstract: The kagome superconductors AV3Sb5 (A=K, Rb, Cs) host multiband superconductivity (SC) within an unconventional charge density wave (CDW) parent phase, yet the relationship between the two remains unresolved. Furthermore, we observe differences in coupling channels between KV3Sb5 and CsV3Sb5. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者在量子极限下对所有沿涡旋的动量数值求解Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程，并计入热展宽与仪器展宽计算局域态密度，以此描述隧道谱跨越Lifshitz转变的演化；同时把屏蔽后的电子相互作用投影到填充的微带上，得到Luttinger参数以刻画核心中的一维费米气体，并类比量子线及量子霍尔边缘态中的三粒子碰撞过程分析准粒子弛豫率。

Conclusion: 磁场可驱动涡旋核中最低Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon微带穿过费米能级的Lifshitz转变，形成自旋极化的一维费米海。隧道谱在阈值场B*附近表现出鲜明的零偏压电导极大及一维van Hove奇点特征，零偏压信号的空间自旋结构在中心与周围环之间发生交换。涡旋核中的一维费米气具有接近1的Luttinger参数，呈弱相互作用行为，其准粒子弛豫通过三粒子碰撞实现，与量子线和量子霍尔边缘态类似。

Abstract: The Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon states bound to a vortex line in a clean type-II superconductor form one-dimensional minibands that disperse along the vortex. The lowest miniband is separated from the Fermi level by a minigap $E_0\simΔ^2/E_F$. A magnetic field shifts the minibands of the two spin projections in opposite directions. At the threshold field $B^*$ the Zeeman energy equals $E_0$. Estimates for FeSe place $B^*$ at a few tesla. The lowest miniband of one spin projection then crosses the Fermi level, and a spin-polarized one-dimensional Fermi sea forms inside the vortex core. We develop the theory of tunneling spectra across this Lifshitz transition. We solve the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations numerically in the quantum limit for all momenta along the vortex and compute the local density of states with thermal and instrumental broadening. The zero-bias conductance at the vortex center is absent below $B^*$. It has a maximum near $B^*$ with the inverse-square-root field dependence of a one-dimensional van Hove singularity and decreases at higher fields. The next miniband produces a second onset at a finite distance from the center. The zero-bias signal is carried by one spin projection at the center and by the opposite one in the surrounding ring. We then project the screened electron interaction onto the filled miniband. The resulting Luttinger parameter is close to unity, so the core hosts a weakly interacting one-dimensional Fermi gas. Its quasiparticles relax through three-particle collisions in analogy with thermalization in quantum wires and at quantum Hall edges. The corresponding relaxation rates are established analytically.

</details>


### [68] [Eigenvalue-based computation of Richardson pairing spectra and finite-size thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31531)
*Grzegorz Biskowski,Marcin R. Piatek,Franco Ferrari*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种求解简化BCS Richardson模型的数值流程，综合了基于本征值的Bethe方程、Baxter多项式重构与复根求解，并通过对不同单粒子能谱（等间距、二次型、类氢）计算随配对强度变化的有限系统热力学可观测量，揭示了单粒子能级间距对有限尺寸热响应（如热容双峰）的塑造作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一套稳健且公开可用的数值流程，以精确求解简化BCS Richardson模型的完整 seniority-zero 谱，从而为理解有限系统中单粒子能级结构如何影响配对与热力学行为（尤其是热容峰值等有限尺寸效应）提供工具。

Method: 采用“基于本征值的Bethe方程 + Baxter多项式重构 + 复根求解”的组合方法：用Newton-Raphson迭代求解基于本征值变量的非线性方程组；用线性方程组求解Baxter多项式系数；用带多项式降阶（deflation）的Laguerre迭代提取rapidities。将该流程应用于等间距、二次型及类氢的非简并单粒子能谱，并随配对强度变化，通过完整的 seniority-zero 谱计算正则系综可观测量，并与精确对角化基准对比。

Result: 在具有类氢谱的八能级、四对系统中，热容在弱耦合下出现两个极大值；随耦合增强，低温峰向上移动、高温峰向下移动，高温峰在所用单位下约 g≈0.03515 处不再作为独立的局部极大值。相比之下，等间距谱在所展示的耦合区间内仅有一个主导极大值，说明单粒子能级间距会显著影响有限尺寸的热响应形态。

Conclusion: 该数值流程能够有效求解简化BCS Richardson模型并用于有限系统热力学研究；单粒子能级间距对有限尺寸热响应具有决定性影响。代码已公开（C++），但在病态构型及更大系统规模下仍可能存在数值不稳定性。

Abstract: We present a numerical workflow for the reduced BCS Richardson model, combining the eigenvalue-based Bethe equations, Baxter-polynomial reconstruction, and complex root finding. Newton-Raphson iteration solves the nonlinear equations for the eigenvalue-based variables; a linear system yields the polynomial coefficients, and Laguerre iteration with polynomial deflation extracts the rapidities. We apply the procedure to equally spaced, quadratic, and hydrogen-like non-degenerate single-particle spectra as the pairing strength varies. For finite systems, we calculate canonical observables from the complete seniority-zero spectrum and check benchmark spectra against exact diagonalization. In an eight-level, four-pair system with a hydrogen-like spectrum, the heat capacity has two maxima at weak coupling. With increasing coupling, the low-temperature maximum shifts upward and the high-temperature maximum downward; the latter ceases to be a distinct local maximum near $g\simeq0.03515$ in the units used. The equally spaced benchmark instead has a single dominant maximum over the coupling interval displayed for that spectrum. This comparison illustrates how single-particle level spacings can shape finite-size thermal response. The C++ code is publicly available; numerical instabilities remain possible near ill-conditioned configurations and at larger sizes.

</details>


### [69] [Majorana flat bands from mirror-particle-hole antisymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31597)
*Justin Schirmann,Martin Rodriguez-Vega,Alexander Cerjan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文证明：在完全带隙的 D 类超导体中，即使没有手征对称性和时间反演，只要超导能隙在镜面反射下为奇且沿边缘平移对称性保持，就能出现横跨整个边缘布里渊区的 Majorana 平带。镜面与粒子-空穴共轭结合形成一个平方为 +1 的反幺正反对称，固定每个边缘动量的 Kitaev 指标，从而保证平带存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为 Majorana 平带（整个边缘布里渊区的零模）依赖于手征对称性。作者希望突破这一限制，探究在无手征对称性、无时间反演的 D 类超导体中，是否以及如何由其他对称性（镜面与平移）保护边缘平带，并寻找可实验实现的最小模型。

Method: 利用镜面反射（超导能隙为奇）与粒子-空穴共轭结合形成的反幺正反对称（平方为 +1 且保持边缘动量），论证它固定每个固定边缘动量切片的 Kitaev 指标；在带隙体态中该切片指标不随边缘动量变化，从而零模构成横跨整个边缘布里渊区的平带。构造一个四带模型（具有面内交换场下 Rashba 耦合的螺旋 p 波超导体），该模型的对称性不可再约去多余项；在镜面线上两个镜面扇区是精确的 Kitaev 链，由此得到闭式相图。用谱局域化子（spectral localizer）在实空间、有限尺寸样品上直接探测所有指标，并构造磁性吸附原子在三重态超导体上的最小模型。

Result: 模型除平带外，还出现每根线两端各有两个 Majorana 的相（尽管其 D 类指标平凡），以及具有 Majorana 角模的二阶拓扑相。谱局域化子在实空间直接探测出所有这些指标：在两种宽度的开口矩形薄片上，尽管平移对称性被破坏，仍复现出相图，且在无序强度达体隙数倍时读数仍保持；在节点区域其读数依赖于薄片尺寸。无边缘平移对称性时，对称性至多保护 Majorana 宇称，作者量化了反对称特有的剩余特征。磁性吸附原子在三重态超导体上的最小模型实现了平带相与二阶拓扑相。

Conclusion: 在完全带隙的 D 类超导体中，镜面奇超导配对与沿边缘平移对称性共同构成的反幺正反对称可保护边缘 Majorana 平带，无需手征对称性或时间反演；同时该框架还容纳平凡 D 类指标的 Majorana 对相、二阶 Majorana 角模相，并能通过谱局域化子对有限尺寸、无序、无平移情形进行实空间诊断与实验验证。

Abstract: Majorana flat bands, zero modes across the whole edge Brillouin zone, usually rest on chiral symmetry. We show that a fully gapped class-D superconductor can host such a flat band without chiral symmetry or time reversal, provided its superconducting gap is odd under a mirror and translation along the edge is preserved. Combined with particle--hole conjugation, the mirror forms an antiunitary antisymmetry that squares to $+1$ and preserves the momentum along the edge, so it fixes the Kitaev index of every slice of fixed edge momentum. In a gapped bulk, this slice index cannot change with the edge momentum, and the edge zero modes form a band across the whole edge Brillouin zone. We construct a four-band model with exactly these symmetries, a helical $p$-wave superconductor with Rashba coupling in an in-plane exchange field, from which no term can be removed without restoring an additional symmetry. On the mirror lines, its two mirror sectors are exact Kitaev chains. This yields a closed-form phase diagram. Besides the flat band, the model hosts a phase with two Majoranas at each end of a wire, although its class-D index is trivial, and a second-order phase with Majorana corner modes. The spectral localizer detects all these indices in real space, directly on finite-size samples as relevant for experiments. On open rectangular flakes of two widths, it reproduces the phase diagram even though translational symmetry is broken. In the nodal regions, its reading depends on the flake size, so they are left unread. The readings persist under disorder several times the bulk gap. Without translation along the edge, the symmetries protect no more than Majorana parities, and we quantify the signatures that remain specific to the antisymmetry. A minimal model of magnetic adatoms on a triplet superconductor realizes the flat-band and second-order phases.

</details>


### [70] [Superconductivity with valley-imbalanced Fermi surfaces in thick rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31861)
*Le Liu,Jian Zhao,Cheng Xu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yang Zhang,Weibo Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了在hBN对齐的17层菱方石墨烯中观察到面内磁场增强的超导现象，并提出了一种谷不平衡、自旋极化的母体金属态和磁子辅助的同自旋谷间配对机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋极化金属中的超导性挑战了传统的相反自旋配对图像，尤其在磁场反而增强超导的情况下。石墨烯中额外的谷自由度扩展了可能的配对通道，因此理解母体金属的谷占据对揭示这类超导机制至关重要。

Result: 正常态量子振荡显示不等费米口袋，表明谷不平衡、自旋极化的母体金属；计算表明母体电子口袋主要局域于底面，并在新费米口袋出现时被静电屏蔽大体保留；当载流子向hBN对齐表面极化时超导被抑制，此处母体能带发生强莫尔重构；相邻的非线性金属响应连续演化为超导临界电流特征，并在超导转变温度以上持续存在。

Conclusion: 谷不平衡与面内磁场增强超导的结合支持一种唯象的磁子辅助同自旋谷间配对情景；这些发现为谷不平衡金属中的配对提供了实验约束，并为通过对母体电子态的层选择性调控来检验磁配对机制开辟了途径。

Abstract: Superconductivity in spin-polarized metals challenges conventional opposite-spin pairing, particularly when a magnetic field strengthens rather than suppresses it. In graphene, the additional valley degree of freedom broadens the possible pairing channels, making the valley occupation of the parent metal central to understanding such superconductivity. Here we report in-plane-field-enhanced superconductivity in hBN-aligned 17-layer rhombohedral graphene. Normal-state quantum oscillations reveal unequal Fermi pockets consistent with a valley-imbalanced, spin-polarized parent metal. Calculations show that the parent electron pocket is predominantly localized on the bottom surface and largely preserved by electrostatic screening as additional Fermi pockets emerge. Superconductivity persists in this regime but is suppressed when carriers are polarized toward the hBN-aligned surface, where the parent bands undergo strong moire reconstruction. An adjacent nonlinear metallic response evolves continuously into superconducting critical-current features and persists above the superconducting transition temperature. The combination of valley imbalance and in-plane-field-enhanced superconductivity motivates a phenomenological magnon-assisted equal-spin intervalley pairing scenario. These findings provide experimental constraints on pairing in valley-imbalanced metals and open a route to testing magnetic pairing mechanisms through layer-selective control of the parent electronic states.

</details>


### [71] [Magnetoelectric response to a Néel domain wall in spin-orbit coupled superconducting films: Spontaneous currents, vortex pinning, and vortex-antivortex nucleation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32379)
*P. J. F. Cavalcanti,M. D. Croitoru,A. I. Buzdin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究在具有界面 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合的铁磁/超导薄膜中，Néel 畴壁所诱导的超导响应，解析推导了平衡相位、自发电流、磁场及涡旋-畴壁相互作用，并展示了畴壁可作为涡旋选择性钉扎源及诱导涡旋-反涡旋对的成核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋轨道耦合超导薄膜中，磁织构（特别是 Néel 畴壁）通过磁电耦合如何诱导自发平衡电流、钉扎涡旋以及促发涡旋-反涡旋对成核，为超导磁电调控提供理论框架。

Method: 先以周期性谐波摆线（cycloid）作为可解析处理的参考问题，研究磁电耦合对 Tc 附近序参量的修正及 Tc 以下的相位响应、Pearl 屏蔽和涡旋相互作用；随后针对单个 Néel 畴壁推导平衡相位分布、自发电流、磁场和涡旋-畴壁相互作用的解析表达式。

Result: Néel 畴壁表现为扩展的磁电源，产生由畴壁宽度与有效屏蔽长度竞争所控制的选择性涡旋钉扎势；足够强的磁电耦合可在无外场条件下使束缚的涡旋-反涡旋对在能量上优于无涡旋态。

Conclusion: 本文建立了自旋轨道耦合超导薄膜中 Néel 畴壁导致的磁电涡旋钉扎和涡旋-反涡旋成核的解析理论，为相关现象的理解提供了分析框架。

Abstract: We study the superconducting response induced by a Néel domain wall in a thin superconducting film proximity-coupled to a ferromagnetic layer in the presence of interfacial Rashba spin-orbit coupling. The combined action of the magnetic texture and broken inversion symmetry generates a magnetoelectric coupling that induces spontaneous equilibrium currents. To establish the basic response to a continuously rotating magnetic texture, we first consider a periodic harmonic cycloid as an analytically tractable reference problem. Near Tc, this reference problem illustrates how the magnetoelectric coupling modifies the superconducting order parameter, while below Tc it provides a simple setting for describing the phase response, Pearl screening, and the interaction with a vortex. We then turn to the main problem of this work, a single Néel domain wall, and derive analytical expressions for its equilibrium phase profile, spontaneous current, magnetic field, and vortex-wall interaction energy.We show that the Néel wall acts as an extended magnetoelectric source and produces a vorticity-selective pinning landscape controlled by the competition between the wall width and the effective screening length. We further demonstrate that sufficiently strong magnetoelectric coupling can render a bound vortex-antivortex pair energetically favorable relative to the vortex-free state, even in the absence of an applied magnetic field. Our results provide an analytical framework for understanding magnetoelectric vortex pinning and vortex-antivortex nucleation by Néel domain walls in spin-orbit coupled superconducting films.

</details>


### [72] [From Fermions to Vortices: Microscopic Simulations of Phase Ordering in a d-Wave Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32916)
*Ali Rayat,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于绝热变分原理（AVP）的微观框架，用于直接从费米子哈密顿量描述耗散型超导相有序化，并在二维 d 波超导体中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从微观费米子哈密顿量出发描述“浴”（环境）诱导的超导相位有序化一直是一项难题。现有方法多依赖唯象的 Ginzburg-Landau 自由能泛函，无法保留超导序参量演化过程中的微观电子结构，因此需要一个既能刻画耗散演化、又能保留微观电子结构的框架。

Method: 作者采用最近提出的绝热变分原理（AVP），核心构造是将演化的、空间非均匀的键配对场映射到受约束的 Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）态上，并以相关的拉格朗日乘子确定热力学驱动力。该方法在不假定唯象 Ginzburg-Landau 自由能泛函的前提下，实现超导序参量的耗散演化并同时保留其微观电子结构。随后从无序配对构型出发，解析了涡旋-反涡旋湮灭所导致的相位相干性的涌现，以及局域电子谱的伴随演化。

Result: 从无序配对构型出发，该框架成功解析出相位相干性的涌现，表现为涡旋-反涡旋湮灭，并伴随局域电子谱的演化。涡旋衰变和动力学标度完美复原了二维相位有序化的特征动力学，为框架的正确性提供了非平凡基准。

Conclusion: 该框架与求解器无关（solver-independent），为研究具有交织条纹序与配对密度波序的超导体中的耗散有序化与亚稳性开辟了一条微观研究的途径。

Abstract: Describing bath-induced superconducting phase ordering directly from a microscopic fermionic Hamiltonian remains a formidable challenge. Here we develop a microscopic framework for this problem using the recently introduced adiabatic variational principle (AVP) and demonstrate its application to a two-dimensional $d$-wave superconductor. The central construction maps evolving, spatially inhomogeneous bond-pairing fields onto constrained Bogoliubov-de Gennes states, with the associated Lagrange multipliers determining the thermodynamic driving forces. This enables dissipative evolution of the superconducting order while retaining its microscopic electronic structure, without assuming a phenomenological Ginzburg-Landau free-energy functional. Starting from disordered pairing configurations, we resolve the emergence of phase coherence through vortex-antivortex annihilation and the accompanying evolution of local electronic spectra. Vortex decay and dynamical scaling recover the characteristic kinetics of two-dimensional phase ordering, providing a nontrivial benchmark of the framework. The solver-independent AVP formulation opens a route to microscopic studies of dissipative ordering and metastability in superconductors with intertwined stripe and pair-density-wave orders.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维超导体在磁场作用下能否被驱动为拓扑超导态。自洽求解BdG方程发现，对于具有Rashba自旋轨道耦合的自旋费米子，磁场产生的超导态几乎处处具有偶数BdG陈数；而当引入Anderson型在位无序后，无序会加宽并混合朗道能级，在陈数转变区域产生拓扑超导电性的逾渗，形成"拓扑Anderson超导态"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇整数BdG陈数的二维拓扑超导体因预言可容纳Majorana零模而备受关注。磁场能产生非零陈数的朗道能级，因此一个自然的问题是：具有Rashba自旋轨道耦合的自旋费米子构成的常规二维超导体，能否通过施加磁场而被驱动为拓扑超导态。

Method: 对在位吸引相互作用，自洽求解Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）方程，得到随化学势等参数变化的相图；在此基础上引入Anderson型在位无序，研究无序对朗道能级的加宽与混合效应，考察陈数转变区域中拓扑超导电性的空间逾渗行为。

Result: 无无序时，相图几乎处处由偶数整数BdG陈数$C_{\rm BdG}$的超导态组成，陈数随化学势等参数变化而从一个值跳变到另一个。引入Anderson在位无序后，在陈数转变区域出现拓扑超导电性逾渗的状态，即拓扑Anderson超导态。

Conclusion: 拓扑Anderson超导态为"无序可产生拓扑非平庸态"提供了一个新的实例，表明Anderson型无序在朗道能级转变区域可以诱导出拓扑超导电性的逾渗。

Abstract: There is much interest in two-dimensional superconductors with odd integer values of the Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) Chern number $C_{\rm BdG}$, often termed topological superconductors, as these are predicted to harbor Majorana zero modes. Because a magnetic field produces Landau levels (LL) with nonzero Chern numbers, it is natural to ask if a conventional two-dimensional superconductor of spinful fermions with Rashba spin-orbit coupling can be driven topological by application of a magnetic field. Self-consistent solutions of the BdG equations for an on-site attractive interaction produce a phase diagram consisting almost everywhere of superconducting states with even integer values of $C_{\rm BdG}$, transitioning from one value to another as parameters such as the chemical potential are varied. We predict that introducing Anderson-type on-site disorder, which broadens as well as mixes LLs, produces, in the transition region, states wherein topological superconductivity percolates. This topological Anderson superconducting state provides another example where disorder can produce topologically nontrivial states.

</details>

Abstract: Multistable systems can store and process information, yet their elements usually only burn the drive energy. Here, we build an automaton whose elements actively convert heat into electrical power. Heat alone stabilizes the states at zero bias, and each state routes generation and dissipation to different junctions. The elements, gap-asymmetric superconducting tunnel junctions, deliver up to 2.6 pW at either polarity under fixed heating and support a magnetic-field-tunable Josephson current. Two junctions sharing a heated island split the bias hysteretically and, even at zero bias, lock into opposite voltages encoding the sweep direction. With a load and current bias, the automaton accesses states spanning junction polarity combinations, including zero voltage; the magnetic field sets which are available and the bias history selects the occupied one.

### [75] [Strain-enabled dynamical magnetoelectric coupling in BaFe$_{12}$O$_{19}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31413)
*ChanJu You,Jayanti Higgins,Zhiren He,Yilin Evan Li,Harikrishnan KP,Sören Buchenau,Christo Guguschev,Mario Brützam,Jiaqiang Yan,David Mandrus,David A. Muller,Darrell G. Schlom,Guru Khalsa,Ankit S. Disa*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学激发是创造非平衡物相的有力手段，但其操控路径受限于平衡态体系的对称性与相互作用。作者希望突破这一限制，通过原子结构工程设计新的光学操控通道，从而获得常规方法无法触及的隐藏动力学性质。

Method: 将共振振动（中红外声子）激发与外延应变相结合，作用于钡六铁氧体（BaFe12O19，BaM）应变薄膜。采用时间分辨二次谐波产生（SHG）与磁光克尔效应（MOKE）测量，结合结构分析与基于第一性原理的理论计算，研究光激发后的铁电极化与磁矩变化及其微观机制。

Result: 在应变BaM薄膜中，激发中红外声子后观察到增强的铁电极化与磁矩减弱，呈现光诱导、室温的多铁态；而体材料无此效应。理论表明，该效应源于应变导致的Fe3+离子位移，破坏了局域对称性，从而催生动力学磁电耦合。强晶格激发与应变诱导的对称性破缺二者缺一不可。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过原子结构工程可以重新设计光学操控路径，访问隐藏的非平衡现象，为设计常规途径无法实现的非平衡物态提供了重要范例。

Abstract: Optical excitation has emerged as a powerful tool for manipulating and creating non-equilibrium phases of matter. Yet, the pathways available for optical control are intrinsically limited by the symmetries and interactions of the equilibrium system. Here, we demonstrate that these pathways can be redesigned through atomic-structural engineering to access hidden dynamical properties. By combining resonant vibrational excitation with epitaxial strain, we realize a light-induced, room-temperature multiferroic state in strained thin films of the technologically relevant hexagonal ferrimagnet, barium hexaferrite (BaFe$_{12}$O$_{19}$, BaM). Time-resolved second harmonic generation and magneto-optical Kerr effect measurements reveal an enhanced ferroelectric polarization together with a weakening of the magnetic moment upon exciting mid-infrared phonons in strained BaM thin films, but not in the bulk material. Structural analysis and first-principles-based theory show that this effect is enabled by strain-generated displacement of Fe$^{3+}$ ions, which break the local symmetry and facilitate an emergent dynamical magnetoelectric coupling. Both the strong lattice excitation and strain-induced symmetry breaking are essential for enabling this dynamical coupling, promising an important template for designing otherwise inaccessible non-equilibrium phenomena.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在获得三维各向同性两相复合材料中电导率与剪切模量之间交叉性质的更紧界限，并检验这些界限的可达性，探索是否存在能够同时接近两相性质极限的微结构设计。

Result: 得到迄今为止最紧的交叉性质界限；联合性能最佳的设计一致呈现双连续、三重周期极小曲面（triply periodic minimal surface）状微结构，但与转换法界限之间仍存在一个虽小但显著的差距。

Conclusion: 转换法能够提供目前最紧的电导率—剪切模量交叉界限；最优微结构表现为三重周期极小曲面状双连续结构，但理论界限尚未完全可达，提示该界限可能仍非最优，或存在尚未发现的设计。

Abstract: We compute conductivity--shear-modulus cross-property bounds in three-dimensional isotropic two-phase composites using the translation method, and obtain the tightest bounds reported to date. To assess attainability, we perform unit-cell topology optimization on thousands of distinct initial structures and compare optimized designs to the isotropic bounds using an effective-medium approximation that models the macroscopic material as an isotropic assembly of randomly oriented grains. The best jointly performing designs are consistently bicontinuous, triply periodic minimal surface-like microstructures; however, a small but notable gap to the translation bound remains.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The electrical degradation and time-dependent current behavior of vertical $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ Schottky barrier diodes (SBDs) subjected to energetic Au-ion irradiation were systematically investigated. Two successive irradiation exposures were conducted under a constant reverse bias of $-100$ V to evaluate the effects of heavy-ion irradiation on device performance and transient current response. Following irradiation, the devices exhibited degraded forward conduction, increased specific on-resistance, enhanced reverse leakage current, and reduced apparent carrier concentration. During irradiation, pronounced increases in leakage current and transient current spikes were observed. Following each beam termination, the leakage current exhibited an initial rapid decay followed by a slower relaxation process. Single-exponential fitting identified distinct fast and slow relaxation components, while a double-exponential model provided a more comprehensive description of the overall current decay. After the second irradiation exposure, the characteristic time constants of the fast and slow relaxation processes increased from 33.50 to 40.64 s and from 190.35 to 282.29 s, respectively, indicating prolonged recovery dynamics under repeated irradiation. A charge-trapping-based model is proposed to explain the irradiation-induced current enhancement and subsequent fast and slow relaxation processes through modulation and gradual recovery of the effective Schottky tunneling barrier. These findings provide insights into the relationship between heavy-ion-induced electrical degradation and time-dependent current relaxation in $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ SBDs.

</details>

Abstract: We studied the photocarrier dynamics in $γ$-InSe using femtosecond transient reflectance spectroscopy. By measuring the dependence of the differential reflectance on the injected carrier density, we established an empirical relation between the optical response and carrier density, allowing quantitative analysis of the recombination dynamics. The carrier decay exhibits a strong density dependence, indicating a substantial contribution from nonlinear recombination. At high carrier densities, the dynamics are dominated by a second-order recombination process, with a rate constant of $3.8\times10^{-6}~\mathrm{cm^3\,s^{-1}}$. At low densities, the decay becomes approximately exponential, with a carrier lifetime of about 7 ps. We also resolved an initial rise of the band-edge response with a time constant of less than 1 ps, which is attributed to hot-carrier cooling. These results reveal the competition between first-order and second-order recombination in $γ$-InSe and provide quantitative insights into its ultrafast photocarrier dynamics.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光催化水分解制氢面临光生电子-空穴快速复合的瓶颈，范德华异质结构虽可抑制复合，但交错带隙异质结构中电荷转移究竟走type-II还是直接Z-scheme机制难以确定。因此迫切需要系统识别GeC/ReS₂异质结构中占主导的电荷转移机制，并评估其光催化全解水性能。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）系统研究GeC/ReS₂异质结构。首先考察体系的热力学、动力学与热稳定性及其电子结构（间接带隙1.38 eV，增强可见光吸收）；通过能带排列（band alignment）与吉布斯自由能路径分析判断主导的界面电荷转移机制为直接Z-scheme；同时分析析氧反应（OER）与析氢反应（HER）在不同pH范围和不同机制下的反应能垒；最后计算无应变及+6%拉伸应变下的太阳能制氢（STH）效率以评估实际性能。

Result: GeC/ReS₂异质结构具备良好的热力学、动力学和热稳定性，具有1.38 eV间接带隙，显著增强可见光吸收。能带排列与吉布斯自由能分析表明直接Z-scheme为占主导的界面电荷转移机制。OER在4≤pH≤14范围内光照下可自发进行（type-II与Z-scheme均可），但Z-scheme路径能垒更低；type-II机制不适合HER，而Z-scheme机制可在全pH范围（0≤pH≤14）高效驱动HER。无应变时STH效率达41.40%，远超10%商业化阈值；在+6%拉伸应变下进一步提升至60.68%。

Conclusion: Z-scheme应变可调（strain-tunable）的GeC/ReS₂异质结构是一种有前景且高效的全解水光催化剂，其直接Z-scheme机制占主导，可实现全pH范围的全解水并具备优异的STH效率。

Abstract: Photocatalytic water splitting is considered as one of the most promising approaches for the green hydrogen production. But the rapid recombination of photogenerated electron-hole pairs severely inhibits its photocatalytic efficiency. Construction of van der Waals heterostructures is a good way to suppress this recombination. However, in staggered band heterostructures, the charge transfer could occur either through the type-II or the direct Z-scheme mechanism. Therefore it is very important to identify which mechanism is dominant. In the present work, we systematically study the GeC/ReS$_2$ heterostructure using first-principles density functional theory (DFT). The system exhibits good thermodynamic, dynamic and thermal stability and an indirect band gap of 1.38 eV which greatly enhances the visible-light absorption. We find that the direct Z-scheme mechanism is the dominant interfacial charge transfer by analysing band alignment and Gibbs free energy pathways. The oxygen evolution reaction (OER) can be driven spontaneously in the pH range $4 \le \text{pH} \le 14$ under light irradiation for both type-II and Z-scheme pathways but the energy barrier is much lower for the Z-scheme mechanism. Moreover, the type-II mechanism is not suitable for hydrogen evolution reaction (HER), but the Z-scheme mechanism can promote HER well in the entire pH range ($0 \le \text{pH} \le 14$). Remarkably, the strain-free (pristine) condition exhibits a solar-to-hydrogen (STH) efficiency of 41.40\%, well above the 10\% threshold for commercial viability. The STH efficiency is further enhanced to 60.68\% under a tensile strain of +6\%. To summarise, the Z-scheme strain-tunable GeC/ReS$_2$ heterostructure is a promising and efficient photocatalyst for overall water splitting.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文提出用“魔数层”概念来识别难以从三维母体晶体剥离的非范德华二维材料，并以III-V、II-VI、I-VII及Mn基化合物为例，通过第一性原理计算验证其在特定厚度下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔数概念常用于描述特定尺寸范德华团簇的显著稳定性；作者希望将其扩展到非范德华二维材料，以预测从三维晶体中剥离出稳定二维材料的可能性，解决传统上难以识别可合成非范德华二维体系的问题。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，对III-V、II-VI、I-VII和Mn基化合物等体系在不同厚度下计算超额能（excess energies），寻找特定厚度处出现的显著稳定化现象，并分析其与双层蜂窝结构形成的关系。

Result: 计算发现，在特定厚度下存在显著的稳定化，这种稳定化反映了双层蜂窝结构的形成；基于魔数层的稳定性度量能够有效区分实验上已合成的体系与尚未实现的体系。

Conclusion: “魔数层”概念所定义的稳定性度量可作为预测非范德华二维材料能否实现的关键描述符，为筛选和设计可剥离的非范德华二维材料提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A magic number is a specific size of a van der Waals (vdW) cluster that shows pronounced stability compared to adjacent sizes, and therefore magic clusters are preferentially formed in experiments. We apply the ``magic-layer'' concept to identify non-vdW two-dimensional (2D) materials that are difficult to exfoliate from their parent 3D crystals. As a case, we study the stability of thin films in III-V, II-VI, I-VII, and Mn-based compounds using first-principles calculations. By calculating excess energies for various thicknesses, we find significant stabilization reflecting the formation of double-layer honeycomb structures at specific thicknesses. The stability measure based on the magic-layer concept can distinguish the experimentally synthesized systems from the experimentally unrealized systems, which serves as a key descriptor for predicting the realization of non-vdW 2D materials.

</details>


### [81] [Accelerating Equivariant Interatomic Potentials for Molecular Dynamics through Radial tabulation and Kernel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31865)
*Jaekyun Yoo,Suyeon Ju*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: JET-MLIPs 是一个无需重训练的 GPU 后端，通过用分段三次 Hermite 表替换径向 MLP 并将表查找、球谐、张量积和邻居聚合融合为单一 kernel，显著加速并降低 NequIP 类等变 MLIP 的分子动力学计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）能达到接近量子化学的精度，但其计算开销和 GPU 显存占用限制了大时空尺度的分子动力学模拟；即便张量积被加速，NequIP 卷积中每条边的径向权重的计算与存储仍是主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出 JET-MLIPs（Joint-Kernel Equivariant Convolution with Tabulated Radial Weights）：用分段三次 Hermite 查表替换径向多层感知机，并将表查找、球谐函数、张量积（TP）和邻居聚合融合进单一 kernel，力评估过程中消除逐边径向权重和 MLP 中间量的存储；该后端可直接应用于预训练的 NequIP 家族势，无需重训练。

Result: 在含 5632 个原子的液态电解质上，针对 SevenNet、MACE、NequIP-OAM、Nequix 和 EquFlash 共 15 个已发布势，LAMMPS MD 在单张 H200 GPU 上相较各家族现有 TP 加速后端取得 1.5–5.9 倍步长时间加速和 2.4–24 倍峰值显存降低，同时保持单精度（fp32）精度与动力学稳定性。这使得约 10⁴ 原子体系的多纳秒输运性质模拟可在单 GPU 上完成。

Conclusion: JET-MLIPs 通过消除径向权重计算与存储瓶颈，大幅提升等变 MLIP MD 的时空效率，使大尺度、长时间模拟在广泛可用的 GPU 上更易实现，拓宽了等变 MLIP 分子动力学的应用范围。

Abstract: Equivariant machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) achieve near-quantum-chemical accuracy, but their computational cost and GPU memory footprint restrict molecular dynamics (MD) at extended length and time scales. Even when the tensor product (TP) is accelerated, both bottlenecks remain governed by evaluating and storing per-edge radial weights in the NequIP convolution. We introduce JET-MLIPs (Joint-Kernel Equivariant Convolution with Tabulated Radial Weights for MLIP simulation), a GPU backend applicable to pretrained NequIP-family potentials without retraining. JET-MLIPs replaces the radial multilayer perceptron (MLP) with a piecewise cubic Hermite table and fuses table lookup, spherical harmonics, TP and neighbor aggregation into a single kernel, eliminating per-edge radial-weight and MLP-intermediate storage during force evaluation. Across 15 released potentials from SevenNet, MACE, NequIP-OAM, Nequix and EquFlash on a 5632-atom liquid electrolyte, LAMMPS MD attains 1.5-5.9$\times$ step-time speed-ups and 2.4-24$\times$ peak memory reductions relative to each family's existing TP-accelerated backend on a single H200 GPU while preserving single-precision (fp32) accuracy and dynamical stability. These reductions enable multi-nanosecond transport-property simulations of roughly $10^4$-atom systems on a single GPU and broaden access to large-scale equivariant MLIP MD on widely available GPUs.

</details>


### [82] [From Weighted Frontier d-States to Geometric Descriptors for Atomically Dispersed Catalysts at the Molecule-Solid Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32059)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对单原子分散催化剂（M-N/C类）在氧还原反应（ORR）中的电子结构描述提出统一框架：N→P取代的M-N3P体系可提升起始电位，通过"加权前沿d态"（εWFDS）和几何描述符（ζ、M-N3PO）实现对ΔG*OH的高精度预测，并通过火山图分析指导轴向氧配位设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单原子分散催化剂因局域金属轨道与载体能带杂化而呈现"分子-固体过渡"特性，既无法被d带中心描述完全刻画，也无法被前沿轨道图像完整解释。因此需要一个统一的电子-几何框架来描述并优化ORR活性。

Method: 作者对14种金属的M-N4和M-N3P位点开展电子结构计算与ORR热力学分析；建立标度关系将*OOH、*O、*OH吸附能归结为ΔG*OH；提出加权前沿d态（εWFDS）电子描述符（由吸附相关轨道及其占据数构建）与几何描述符ζM-N3P = nd/rM-P（nd为金属d电子数，rM-P为M-P键长）；进一步研究轴向氧配位（M-N3PO），并验证描述符与机器学习原子间势的兼容性。

Result: N→P取代使13个体系的起始电位升高，其中Rh-N3P达0.88 V，与Pt(111)基准相当。M-N4遵循dyz主导的前沿轨道图像，而M-N3P需用加权前沿d态描述：εWFDS与ΔG*OH相关度R²=0.92，优于d带中心（0.83）和前沿轨道（0.64）。几何描述符ζM-N3P预测ΔG*OH的R²达0.96。火山图识别出强结合体系并引导轴向氧配位，使起始电位提升0.70-2.46 V，改造后的M-N3PO描述符预测ΔG*OH的R²=0.91。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了前沿轨道与d带中心两种描述，提供了物理上有依据的电子结构表征（εWFDS）和基于几何的筛选路径（ζ、M-N3PO描述符），适用于与机器学习原子间势相结合的催化剂高通量筛选。

Abstract: Atomically dispersed catalysts exhibit molecule-solid crossover character when localized metal orbitals hybridize with support-derived bands, a regime not fully captured by either a d-band-center description or a frontier-orbital picture. We establish an electronic-to-geometric framework for oxygen reduction across fourteen metals. N-to-P substitution in M-N4 sites raises theoretical onset potentials in 13 systems, with Rh-N3P reaching 0.88 V, comparable to the Pt(111) benchmark. Scaling relations reduce *OOH, *O, and *OH thermodynamics to $Δ$G*OH. M-N4 follows a dyz-dominated frontier-orbital picture, whereas M-N3P requires a weighted frontier d-state representation built from adsorption-relevant orbitals and their occupancies. The resulting $ε$WFDS correlates with $Δ$G*OH at $R^2$ = 0.92, compared with 0.83 for the d-band center and 0.64 for the frontier-orbital. The identified roles of orbital occupancy and local coordination suggest an interpretable geometric descriptor, $ζ$M-N3P = nd/rM-P, that predicts $Δ$G*OH at $R^2$ = 0.96. The ORR volcano analysis identifies strong-binding systems and motivates axial oxygen coordination. This design raises onset potentials by 0.70-2.46 V, and the M-N3PO descriptor predicts $Δ$G*OH at $R^2$ = 0.91. By bridging frontier-orbital and d-band-center descriptions, the framework provides a physically grounded electronic representation and a geometry-based screening route compatible with machine-learned interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [83] [Emergent Electric-Field-Tunable $p$-Wave Magnetism in Twisted Bilayer NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32103)
*Jiaran Su,Jin-Xin Hu,Qi Sun,Dante M. Kennes,Yuhao Fu,Lede Xian,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在21°扭转的NiPS₃双层中，垂直电场可在不改变局域磁构型的情况下重新定向动量空间自旋纹理，并通过第一性原理和线性响应计算揭示了栅压对自旋分裂、自旋电导率及自旋流各向异性的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索垂直电场对动量空间自旋纹理的重新定向机制，并利用扭转NiPS₃双层的对称性（反幺正半平移与层交换二重对称）与共面非平行Néel矢量，寻找在保持磁序前提下调控奇宇称自旋纹理和自旋流各向异性的途径。

Method: 以21°扭转的NiPS₃双层（具有锯齿序和共面、非平行的层间Néel矢量）为对象，进行对称性分析：非相对论极限下反幺正面内半平移导致相反动量处的相反自旋配对，层交换二重操作将主导p波自旋分裂钉扎在晶向方向上；施加垂直层偏压以解除取向约束。采用第一性原理计算研究栅压依赖的自旋分辨等能轮廓重取向与形变，并用基于Wannier的线性响应计算分析场致对角自旋电导率和横向自旋流响应的极性依赖倾斜。

Result: 垂直层偏压在保持反幺正对称性的同时解除了取向钉扎，使自旋分裂矢量出现横向分量；第一性原理显示等能轮廓随栅压发生重取向和形变；线性响应计算揭示出场激活的对角自旋电导率，以及横向自旋流响应角分布随极性发生倾斜。自旋纹理轴、色散轴和输运响应轴是受同一对称性降低支配的不同物理量。

Conclusion: 在共面磁态保持稳定的前提下，垂直层偏压是调控奇宇称自旋纹理和自旋流各向异性的可行途径，为利用栅压控制自旋分裂与自旋流输运提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A perpendicular electric field can reorient a momentum-space spin texture without changing the local magnetic configuration. In this Letter, we investigate this mechanism in a $21^\circ$-twisted $\mathrm{NiPS}_3$ bilayer with zigzag order and coplanar, nonparallel layer Néel vectors. In the ideal nonrelativistic limit, an antiunitary in-plane half-translation enforces opposite-spin pairing at opposite momenta, while a layer-exchanging twofold operation pins the leading $p$-wave spin splitting to a crystallographic direction. A perpendicular layer bias lifts this orientational constraint while preserving the antiunitary symmetry, allowing a transverse component of the splitting vector. First-principles calculations show gate-dependent reorientation and deformation of spin-resolved isoenergy contours. Wannier-based linear-response calculations further reveal field-activated diagonal spin conductivities and a polarity-dependent tilt in the angular profile of the transverse spin-current response. The spin-texture axis, dispersion axes, and transport-response axis are distinct quantities governed by the same symmetry reduction. These results identify layer bias as a route to controlling odd-parity spin textures and spin-current anisotropy, provided that the coplanar magnetic state remains stable.

</details>


### [84] [AI-assisted formal proofs for static and time-dependent density functional theory on lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32370)
*Miguel A. L. Marques,Irene D'Amico,Stefano Pittalis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明在晶格上 Hohenberg-Kohn 定理的反例（共享基态、势能相差超过常数的势）属于零测度的疏集，并将该结论推广至 Kohn-Sham 势、自旋密度泛函理论和含时密度泛函理论，且所有定理均已在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hohenberg-Kohn 定理在晶格上不成立：Penz 与 van Leeuwen 给出了两个势相差超过常数却共享同一基态的反例。这动摇了晶格密度泛函理论的严格基础。作者希望刻画这类反例的“稀少性”或“通有性”——即从测度和拓扑上证明它们是非典型的，从而为晶格 DFT 的适用性提供理论保障，并以计算机形式化验证确保结论的可靠性。

Method: 作者考虑任意连通晶格上的无自旋费米子，填充数既不空也不满，跃迁振幅同号，并允许任意密度-密度相互作用。通过分析共享基态的势所满足的约束条件，证明这些势构成闭合、无处稠密且勒贝格测度为零的集合；非简并共享基态的密度构成余维数至少为二的集合。进而将论证推广到 Kohn-Sham 势和自旋密度泛函理论（其中磁场均匀平移是唯一的额外自由度）。对于含时密度泛函理论，作者证明并非每个连通晶格的基态都能作为密度-势映射的初态，并构造/引用反例。此外还给出含时 Kohn-Sham 势局部存在性的形式证明，以及若干已知结果（可容许初态的局部存在唯一性、时间延拓至容许态集合边界、正则化 Kohn-Sham 迭代的收敛性）的形式化证明。所有定理与命题均在证明助手 Lean 4 中形式化并计算机检查。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 与相差超过常数的势共享基态的势构成闭合、无处稠密、零勒贝格测度集合；(2) 非简并共享基态的密度构成余维数至少为二的集合；(3) 结论对 Kohn-Sham 势及自旋 DFT 成立，后者仅额外允许磁场均匀平移；(4) 在晶格含时 DFT 中，并非每个连通晶格基态都能作为密度-势映射初态，正方晶格上两费米子基态为反例；(5) 然而对几乎所有静态势，每个实基态（含自旋分辨密度情形）都可作为初态；(6) 对几乎所有静态 Kohn-Sham 势，含时 Kohn-Sham 势局部（在时间上）存在；(7) 全部定理在 Lean 4 中形式化并经计算机核验。

Conclusion: 本文从测度与拓扑视角澄清了晶格 Hohenberg-Kohn 定理失效的程度：反例确实存在，但极其“稀少”（零测度且无处稠密），因此晶格密度泛函理论在通有意义上依然稳固。结论进一步覆盖 Kohn-Sham、自旋和含时情形，且全部结果经 Lean 4 形式化验证，极大增强了结论的可信度。

Abstract: The Hohenberg-Kohn theorem fails on lattices, where two potentials that differ by more than a constant can share a ground state. We prove that such potentials are exceptional. We consider spinless fermions on any connected lattice, at any filling other than empty or full, with hopping amplitudes of a common sign and any density-density interaction. The potentials whose ground state is shared with a potential differing by more than a constant lie in a closed, nowhere dense set of zero Lebesgue measure. The densities of non-degenerate shared ground states form a set of codimension at least two. The statement about the potentials also holds for the Kohn-Sham potential and for spin-density functional theory, where a uniform shift of the magnetic field is the only additional freedom. In time-dependent density functional theory on lattices, we show that not every ground state of a connected lattice can serve as the initial state of the density-potential map. A counterexample is the ground state of two fermions on a square that Penz and van Leeuwen found as a counterexample to the Hohenberg-Kohn theorem. For almost every static potential, however, every real ground state can serve as an initial state, also for spin-resolved densities. We also give formal proofs that the time-dependent Kohn-Sham potential exists, locally in time, for almost every static Kohn-Sham potential, and of known results: local existence and uniqueness of the time-dependent potential for an admissible initial state, continuation in time up to the boundary of the set of admissible states, and the convergence properties of a regularized Kohn-Sham iteration. All theorems and propositions are formalized in the proof assistant Lean 4 and checked by computer.

</details>


### [85] [Continuum model for disconnection structures of hetero-interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32412)
*Luchan Zhang,Xiaoxue Qin,Dongsong Tao,Jian Han,David J. Srolovitz,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续介质模型，通过界面能最小化并结合广义Frank-Bilby方程约束，预测异质界面的位错（disconnection）网络、界面倾角和错配角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质界面（hetero-interfaces）作为不同材料之间的平面缺陷，通过位错（disconnection，结构台阶与位错特征的组合）网络来调节晶格失配。然而，缺乏能同时预测位错网络、界面倾角与错配角等界面结构的连续介质模型，因此需要发展一种统一的建模方法。

Method: 建立基于界面能最小化的连续介质模型，对所有可能的位错模式进行优化，并以广义Frank-Bilby方程作为界面结构约束，该方程描述跨界面的晶格对应关系，同时包含位错的位错分量与台阶分量以及界面倾角和错配角。随后对三种异质界面进行数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟确定了ZrO₂中α/β（正交/单斜）界面、Ti中α/β异质界面以及Au/Pd(001)扭转-失配界面三种异质界面的平衡构型。与实验、原子模拟及理论预测的对比表明，该连续介质模型能出色预测异质界面的结构，包括位错网络以及倾角和错配角。系统分析还揭示了异质界面结构性质对倾角和错配角的功能依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该连续介质模型能够准确预测多种异质界面的位错网络、界面倾角与错配角，并揭示界面结构对取向关系的依赖规律，为异质界面结构设计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Hetero-interfaces, as planar defects between dissimilar materials, accommodate the lattice misfit through networks of line defects of disconnections (a combination of structural ledges/step and dislocation characters). We develop a continuum model that predicts the hetero-interface structures, including disconnection networks, the interface inclination and misorientation angles. The model is based on the interfacial energy minimization with respect to all possible disconnection modes, subject to the interface structure constraint of the generalized Frank-Bilby equation that describes the lattice correspondence across the interface including both dislocation and step characters of disconnections and the inclination and misorientation angles. Numerical simulations are performed to determine the equilibrium configurations of three different types of hetero-interfaces: the $α$/$β$ (orthorhombic/monoclinic) interface in ZrO$_2$, $α$/$β$ hetero-interfaces in Ti and Au/Pd (001) twist-misfit interfaces. Comparisons with experiments, atomistic simulations and theoretical predictions show that our continuum model provides excellent predictions of the hetero-interface structures including disconnection networks and inclination, misorientation angles. Furthermore, systematic analysis reveals the functional dependence of hetero-interface structural properties on inclination and misorientation angles.

Abstract: We extend our previously introduced mean-field bond model from unary fcc lattices to crystals with multiple chemical species and sublattices, accurately predicting finite-temperature thermodynamics without statistical sampling. Our generalization reformulates the model in terms of the Wyckoff positions of the crystal, representing each by one atom, and solving for the bond density of the reference bond of each of its neighbor shells. Bond-bond correlation functions are built from the zero-temperature force constants, allowing the deviation of every bond in the crystal to be expressed through linear functions of the reference bond of a shell. Model predictions are validated against molecular dynamics simulations of unary, binary, and ternary crystals using Morse-type bonding potentials, parametrized for each shell from the force constants of empirical interatomic potentials at several isotropic strains. In each case, the predicted bond densities and thermodynamics agree with the simulations to within their statistical uncertainty. We discuss strategies for combining the model with bonding potentials parametrized from first principles and foundation models to predict the finite-temperature thermodynamics of complex crystals at ab initio accuracy without statistical sampling.
